16:T842,<p>The Veezoo API allows you to interact programmatically with Veezoo, enabling you to integrate Veezoo into your own
applications and workflows.</p>
<h2 id="python-sdk--cli">Python SDK &#x26; CLI</h2>
<p>Install the official Python SDK for a convenient way to use the API from Python or the command line:</p>
<pre><code>pip install veezoo
</code></pre>
<p>See the <a href="https://pypi.org/project/veezoo/">Veezoo Python SDK on PyPI</a> for documentation, examples, and CLI usage.</p>
<h2 id="authentication">Authentication</h2>
<p>All API requests require authentication using an API key. Include your API key in the <code>X-Api-Key</code> request header:</p>
<pre><code>X-Api-Key: &#x3C;Api-Key>
</code></pre>
<p>You can create and manage API keys in Veezoo Admin on the <a href="https://app.veezoo.com/admin/api/keys">API Keys page</a>.</p>
<h2 id="knowledge-graph">Knowledge Graph</h2>
<p>API endpoints which access a Knowledge Graph require the <code>kgId</code> query parameter to specify the ID of the Knowledge
Graph to use. You can list available Knowledge Graphs using the
<a href="#list-knowledge-graphs">List Knowledge Graphs</a> endpoint, or find the ID on the
<em>Configuration</em> page in <a href="https://app.veezoo.com/studio">Veezoo Studio</a>.</p>
<h2 id="rate-limiting">Rate Limiting</h2>
<p>API requests are rate-limited. If you exceed the rate limit, you will receive a <code>429 Too Many Requests</code> error
response. The following table shows the effective rate limits:</p>




















<table><thead><tr><th>Type</th><th>Max. requests / second</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Global rate limit</td><td>25</td><td>Rate limit across all API endpoints</td></tr><tr><td>Per-endpoint rate limit</td><td>10</td><td>Rate limit per individual API endpoint</td></tr></tbody></table>
<h2 id="api-request-ids">API Request IDs</h2>
<p>Veezoo assigns each API request a unique request ID and returns it in the <code>X-Request-Id</code> header of the response.
When encountering a problem, you can include this request ID when contacting Veezoo support for assistance.</p>6:["$","div",null,{"className":"flex min-w-0 flex-1","children":["$","main",null,{"className":"min-w-0 flex-1 px-0 py-10 lg:px-12","children":["$","article",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-[52rem]","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3","children":[["$","h1",null,{"className":"font-display text-[2rem] font-semibold leading-tight tracking-tight text-gray-900","children":"Veezoo API"}],["$","span",null,{"className":"rounded-full border border-accent/30 bg-accent/10 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-accent-dark","children":"beta"}]]}],["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-2.5 text-[15px] leading-7 text-gray-500","children":"The Veezoo public REST API: ask questions, run VQL queries and retrieve data programmatically."}],["$","div",null,{"className":"mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-3.5 py-2","children":[["$","span",null,{"className":"text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-gray-400","children":"Base URL"}],["$","code",null,{"className":"font-mono text-[13px] text-gray-800","children":"https://app.veezoo.com/api"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"className":"docs-prose mt-8","dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$16"}}],["$","section",null,{"className":"mt-12","children":[["$","h2",null,{"id":"endpoints","className":"border-b border-gray-200 pb-2 font-display text-[1.4rem] font-semibold tracking-tight text-gray-900","children":"Endpoints"}],"$L17"]}],["$L18","$L19","$L1a","$L1b","$L1c"]]}]}]}]
